# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-09-04)

## Corpus Check
- 1765 files · ~7,342,338 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 36731 nodes · 90991 edges · 1754 communities (916 shown, 838 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 98% EXTRACTED · 2% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 2251 edges (avg confidence: 0.66)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `c9254dc2`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
